Budget normalization: remove commas but keep currency symbol\n    if '예산' in data:\n        budget = data['예산']\n        # Remove commas but keep the currency symbol\n        data['예산'] = budget.replace(',', '')\n\n    return data\n\ndef parse_date_range(date_str):\n    \"\"\"Parse date range and return start/end dates.\"\"\"\n    # Handle formats like \"2026-08 ~ 2026-10\" or \"2027-01\"\n    # First check for range with ~\n    if '~' in date_str:\n        parts = date_str.split('~')\n        start_str = parts[0].strip()\n        end_str = parts[1].strip()\n\n        # Parse start date (might be YYYY-MM or YYYY-MM-DD)\n        start = parse_partial_date(start_str)\n        end = parse_partial_date(end_str)\n        return start, end\n    else:\n        # Single date (like \"2027-01\") - treat as 1 month\n        date = parse_partial_date(date_str.strip())\n        return date, date\n\ndef parse_partial_date(date_str):\n    \"\"\"Parse partial date string to datetime.\"\"\"\n    date_str = date_str.strip()\n    # Try YYYY-MM-DD\n    try:\n        return datetime.strptime(date_str, '%Y-%m-%d')\n    except ValueError:\n        pass\n    # Try YYYY.MM.DD\n    try:\n        return datetime.strptime(date_str, '%Y.%m.%d')\n    except ValueError:\n        pass\n    # Try YYYY-MM (assume first day)\n    try:\n        return datetime.strptime(date_str, '%Y-%m')\n    except ValueError:\n        pass\n    # Try YYYY.MM (assume first day)\n    try:\n        return datetime.strptime(date_str, '%Y.%m')\n    except ValueError:\n        pass\n    return None\n\ndef calculate_months(start_date, end_date):\n    \"\"\"Calculate month difference between two dates.\"\"\"\n    if start_date is None or end_date is None:\n        return 1\n\n    # Calculate months: (end.year - start.year) * 12 + end.month - start.month\n    months = (end_date.year - start_date.year) * 12 + end_date.month - start_date.month\n\n    # If there's any day component beyond the month boundary, round up\n    if end_date.day > start_date.day or (end_date.day == start_date.day and end_date > start_date):\n        # Check if we have extra days\n        pass  # Keep as is for now, or add +1 if needed\n\n    # Ensure at least 1 month\n    return max(1, months)\n\ndef process_section_xml(xml_path, data):\n    \"\"\"Process a section XML file: replace placeholders and compute month spans.\"\"\"\n    # Parse XML\n    tree = ET.parse(xml_path)\n    root = tree.getroot()\n\n    modified = False\n\n    # Find all paragraphs\n    for para in root.findall(f'{{{HWPML_NS}}}p'):\n        para_modified = False\n\n        # Find all text runs in this paragraph\n        for run in para.findall(f'{{{HWPML_NS}}}run'):\n            text_elem = run.find(f'{{{HWPML_NS}}}t')\n            if text_elem is None:\n                continue\n\n            text = text_elem.text or ''\n            new_text = text\n\n            # Replace all {{key}} placeholders\n            for key, value in data.items():\n                placeholder = f'{{{{{key}}}}}'\n                if placeholder in new_text:\n                    new_text = new_text.replace(placeholder, str(value))\n                    para_modified = True\n\n            #
